11608: async function startSimpleImageMigration() {
11609:     if (SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.running) return;
11610:     SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.running=true; SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.startedAt=new Date().toISOString(); SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.lastError=null;

11614:         await pool.query(`ALTER TABLE character_images ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS mime_type TEXT`);

11617:             missing=await simpleMigrationPass(pass);
11618:             if (!missing.length) break;
11619:             if (pass<3) await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,12000));
11620:         }
11621:         SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.done = !missing.length;
11622:     } catch (e) {
11623:         SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.lastError=String(e?.stack || e?.message || e);
11624:         console.error('[Simple images] migration failed:',e);
11625:     } finally {
11626:         SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.running=false; SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();

11686: function __dleIndex(u) {
11687:     if (!__dleNames) __dleNames = new Map();
11688:     const t = DLE_TARGET_NAMES[u] || [], m = DLE_MASTER_NAMES[u] || [];
11689:     const sig = t.length + '|' + m.length;
11690:     let idx = __dleNames.get(u);
11691:     if (idx && idx.sig === sig) return idx;
11692:     const al = DLE_MASTER_ALIASES[u] || {}, display = new Map();
11693:     for (const x of [...t, ...m]) {
11694:         const xn = normalizeDle(x), c = al[xn] || xn;
11695:         if (c && !display.has(c)) display.set(c, x);
11696:     }
11697:     idx = { sig, display };
11698:     __dleNames.set(u, idx);
11699:     return idx;
11700: }

11702: function dleMasterCanonicalNorm(u, rawName) {

11704:     if (!n) return null;
11705:     const c = (DLE_MASTER_ALIASES[u] || {})[n] || n;
11706:     return __dleIndex(u).display.has(c) ? c : null;
11707: }

881: function getRankLabel(rating) {
882:     for (const tier of RANK_TIERS) {
883:         if (rating >= tier.min && rating <= tier.max) {
884:             return `${tier.name} ${tier.division}`;
885:         }
886:     }
887:     return 'Élite'; // au-dessus de 2499 : classement pur à l'ELO, plus de divisions
888: }

897: async function recordMatchResult(userId, won, eloDelta) {
898:     if (!userId) return;
899:     try {
900:         await pool.query(
901:             `UPDATE users SET
902:                 rating = GREATEST(rating + $1, 0),
903:                 wins = wins + $2,
904:                 losses = losses + $3
905:              WHERE id = $4`,
906:             [eloDelta, won ? 1 : 0, won ? 0 : 1, userId]
907:         );
908:     } catch (err) {
909:         console.error('Erreur mise à jour ELO :', err);
910:     }
911: }

926: // Undercover Normal/Hardcore + Devine la note (même mécanique imposteur/civils)
927: async function applyImpostorModeRanking(room, { impostorsWin, noImpostorRoundWin }) {
928:     const scale = (room.mode === 'undercover' && room.subMode === 'hardcore') ? ELO.hardcore : ELO.normal;
929: 
930:     for (const p of room.players) {
931:         if (!p.userId) continue;
932:         let won;
933:         let eloDelta;
934: 
935:         if (noImpostorRoundWin) {
936:             won = true;
937:             eloDelta = scale.winCivil;
938:         } else if (p.isImpostor) {
939:             won = impostorsWin;
940:             eloDelta = won ? scale.winImpostor : -scale.loss;
941:         } else {
942:             won = !impostorsWin;
943:             eloDelta = won ? scale.winCivil : -scale.loss;
944:         }
945: 
946:         await recordMatchResult(p.userId, won, eloDelta);
947:         notifyProfileUpdate(p.id, p.userId);
948:     }
949: 
950:     // Le classement général existait déjà via l'ELO, mais ces parties n'étaient pas
951:     // enregistrées dans game_results : elles étaient donc absentes du classement par jeu.

18123: // Rareté : persos connus = communes → légendaires / mythiques ; persos moins connus = rares → mythiques
18124: function cardRarityAt(u, idx) {

18438: async function dailyScoreOf(uid, day, game) {

18443: function challResult(game, a, b) { // a = celui qui défie, b = le défié
18444:     if (game === 'dle') { const x = a.attempts || 99, y = b.attempts || 99; return y < x ? 'win' : y > x ? 'lose' : 'draw'; }
18445:     return b.score > a.score ? 'win' : b.score < a.score ? 'lose' : 'draw';
18446: }
18447: async function challList(uid) {

18451: async function challResolve(c, mine) {

18503:     return r;

6988: /* ================= Rolland Garos : logique de partie ================= */
6989: 
6990: // Repère les prénoms/noms de famille partagés par plusieurs personnages (Charlotte, Uchiha,
6991: // Vinsmoke, Ōtsutsuki...). Ces mots seuls ne valident plus une réponse : il faut le nom complet.
6992: function buildAmbiguousTokens(pool) {
6993:     const compteur = {};
6994:     pool.forEach(name => {
6995:         const n = normalizeRG(name);
6996:         const parts = n.split(' ');
6997:         const tokens = new Set([parts[0], parts[parts.length - 1]]);
6998:         tokens.forEach(t => {
6999:             if (!t) return;
7000:             compteur[t] = compteur[t] || new Set();
7001:             compteur[t].add(n);
7002:         });
7003:     });
7004: 
7005:     const ambigus = new Set();
7006:     Object.keys(compteur).forEach(t => {
7007:         // ambigu si le mot désigne plusieurs persos, sauf si c'est le nom complet de l'un d'eux
7008:         const noms = compteur[t];
7009:         if (noms.size > 1 && !noms.has(t)) ambigus.add(t);
7010:     });
7011:     return ambigus;
7012: }
7013: 
7014: function startRollandGaros(room, roomCode) {
7015:     const key = RG_UNIVERSES[room.subMode] ? room.subMode : 'naruto';
7016:     const universe = RG_UNIVERSES[key];
7017:     const pool = rgMasterPoolForUniverse(key, universe.raw);
7018: 
7019:     rgPools[roomCode] = {
7020:         universeKey: key,
7021:         pool,
7022:         usedNorm: new Set(),
7023:         ambigus: buildAmbiguousTokens(pool)
7024:     };
7025: 
7026:     // Vies individuelles : chaque joueur a ses 2 propres vies
7027:     room.players.forEach(p => {
7028:         p.rgLives = 2;
7029:         p.rgAlive = true;
7030:     });
7031: 
7032:     room.status = 'rg_playing';
7033:     room.rg = {
7034:         universeKey: key,
7035:         universeName: universe.name,
7036:         score: 0,
7037:         turnIndex: 0,
7038:         timeLeft: 10,
7039:         found: [],
7040:         poolSize: pool.length,
7041:         winnerName: null
7042:     };
7043: 
7044:     io.to(roomCode).emit('rg_state', room);
7045:     startRgTimer(room, roomCode);
7046: }
7047: 
7048: function startRgTimer(room, roomCode) {
7049:     if (rgTimers[roomCode]) clearInterval(rgTimers[roomCode]);
7050:     if (!room.rg) return;
7051:     room.rg.timeLeft = 10;
7052:     rgTimers[roomCode] = setInterval(() => {
7053:         try {
7054:             if (!rooms[roomCode] || !room.rg || room.status !== 'rg_playing') {
7055:                 clearInterval(rgTimers[roomCode]);
7056:                 delete rgTimers[roomCode];
7057:                 return;
7058:             }
7059:             room.rg.timeLeft--;
7060:             io.to(roomCode).emit('rg_tick', { timeLeft: Math.max(room.rg.timeLeft, 0) });
7061:             if (room.rg.timeLeft <= 0) {
7062:                 clearInterval(rgTimers[roomCode]);
7063:                 delete rgTimers[roomCode];
7064:                 rgLoseLife(room, roomCode, "⏱ Trop lent, un personnage manqué !");
7065:             }
7066:         } catch (e) {
7067:             // Une erreur dans le chrono ne doit jamais tuer le serveur
7068:             console.error('[chrono Rolland Garos]', e);
7069:             clearInterval(rgTimers[roomCode]);
7070:             delete rgTimers[roomCode];
7071:         }
7072:     }, 1000);
7073: }
7074: 
7075: function rgAlivePlayers(room) {
7076:     return room.players.filter(p => p.rgAlive);
7077: }
7078: 
7079: function rgAdvanceTurn(room) {
7080:     if (room.players.length === 0) return;
7081:     if (rgAlivePlayers(room).length === 0) return;
7082:     let idx = room.rg.turnIndex;
7083:     let guard = 0;
7084:     do {
7085:         idx = (idx + 1) % room.players.length;
7086:         guard++;
7087:     } while (!room.players[idx].rgAlive && guard <= room.players.length);
7088:     room.rg.turnIndex = idx;
7089: }

8362:     return promise;
8363: }

8425: for (const [universeKey, profiles] of Object.entries(DLE_STATIC_PROFILES_EXT)) {
8426:     DLE_STATIC_PROFILES[universeKey] = {
8427:         ...(DLE_STATIC_PROFILES[universeKey] || {}),
8428:         ...profiles
8429:     };
8430:     const current = DLE_TARGET_NAMES[universeKey] || [];
8431:     const seen = new Set(current.map(normalizeDle));
8432:     for (const name of Object.keys(profiles)) {
8433:         if (!seen.has(normalizeDle(name))) {
8434:             current.push(name);
8435:             seen.add(normalizeDle(name));
8436:         }
8437:     }
8438:     DLE_TARGET_NAMES[universeKey] = current;
8439: }

11234: function simpleImageNorm(value) {
11235:     return String(value || '')
11236:         .normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
11237:         .toLowerCase().replace(/[’']/g, '')
11238:         .replace(/&/g, ' and ').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
11239:         .replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
11240: }
11241: function simpleImageCompact(value) { return simpleImageNorm(value).replace(/\s+/g, ''); }
11242: function simpleImageRoute(universe, name, version) {
11243:     const v = version ? '&v=' + encodeURIComponent(String(version)) : '';
11244:     return '/api/character-image-file?u=' + encodeURIComponent(universe) + '&n=' + encodeURIComponent(name) + v;
11245: }
11246: function simpleMimeFromPath(p) {
11247:     const x = String(p || '').toLowerCase();
11248:     if (x.endsWith('.png')) return 'image/png';
11249:     if (x.endsWith('.gif')) return 'image/gif';
11250:     if (x.endsWith('.avif')) return 'image/avif';
11251:     if (x.endsWith('.webp')) return 'image/webp';
11252:     return 'image/jpeg';
11253: }
11254: function simpleImageLooksValid(buf) {
11255:     return Buffer.isBuffer(buf) && buf.length >= 700 && buf.length <= 10 * 1024 * 1024;
11256: }
11257: function simpleImageIsPrivateHost(hostname) {
11258:     const h = String(hostname || '').toLowerCase();
11259:     if (!h || h === 'localhost' || h === '::1' || h.endsWith('.local')) return true;
11260:     if (/^127\./.test(h) || /^10\./.test(h) || /^192\.168\./.test(h) || /^169\.254\./.test(h)) return true;
11261:     const m = h.match(/^172\.(\d+)\./); if (m && Number(m[1]) >= 16 && Number(m[1]) <= 31) return true;
11262:     return false;
11263: }
11264: async function simpleFetchImage(url, timeoutMs = 14000) {
11265:     if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(url || ''))) return null;
11266:     let parsed;
11267:     try { parsed = new URL(url); } catch (_) { return null; }
11268:     if (simpleImageIsPrivateHost(parsed.hostname)) return null;
11269:     const ac = new AbortController();
11270:     const t = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), timeoutMs);
11271:     try {
11272:         const r = await fetch(url, {
11273:             redirect: 'follow', signal: ac.signal,
11274:             headers: { 'User-Agent':'ANIME-GAME/1.0', 'Accept':'image/avif,image/webp,image/apng,image/*,*/*;q=0.8' }
11275:         });
11276:         if (!r.ok) return null;
11277:         const mime = String(r.headers.get('content-type') || '').split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase();
11278:         if (!mime.startsWith('image/')) return null;
11279:         const bytes = Buffer.from(await r.arrayBuffer());
11280:         if (!simpleImageLooksValid(bytes)) return null;
11281:         return { bytes, mime };
11282:     } catch (_) { return null; }
11283:     finally { clearTimeout(t); }
11284: }
11285: function simpleScoreName(candidate, wanted) {
11286:     const a = simpleImageNorm(candidate), b = simpleImageNorm(wanted);
11287:     if (!a || !b) return 0;
11288:     if (a === b) return 100;
11289:     if (simpleImageCompact(a) === simpleImageCompact(b)) return 98;
11290:     if (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a)) {
11291:         const sm = Math.min(a.length,b.length), lg = Math.max(a.length,b.length);
11292:         if (sm >= 4) return 83 + Math.round(14 * sm / lg);
11293:     }
11294:     const A = new Set(a.split(' ')), B = new Set(b.split(' ')); let common = 0;
11295:     for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) common++;
11296:     return Math.round((common / Math.max(A.size,B.size,1)) * 82);
11297: }

11341: async function simpleCatalogue() {
11342:     try {
11343:         const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/character-catalog`, { headers:{Accept:'application/json'} });
11344:         const d = await r.json();
11345:         if (r.ok && d?.ok && Array.isArray(d.characters) && d.characters.length) {
11346:             const map = new Map();
11347:             for (const raw of d.characters) {
11348:                 const u = String(raw?.u || raw?.universe || '').trim();
11349:                 const name = cleanImageCharacterName(String(raw?.name || '').trim());
11350:                 if (!u || !name) continue;
11351:                 const k = `${u}|${normalizeImageKey(name)}`;
11352:                 if (!map.has(k)) map.set(k,{u,name,anime:String(raw?.anime || ARC_UNIVERSE_ANIME?.[u] || u),img:String(raw?.img || raw?.imageUrl || raw?.originalImg || '')});
11353:                 else if (!map.get(k).img && (raw?.img || raw?.imageUrl || raw?.originalImg)) map.get(k).img = String(raw.img || raw.imageUrl || raw.originalImg);
11354:             }
11355:             return [...map.values()];
11356:         }
11357:     } catch (_) {}
11358:     const out = [];
11359:     try {
11360:         for (const [u,b] of Object.entries(STATIC_CHAR_IMAGES || {})) {
11361:             if (!b || typeof b !== 'object') continue;
11362:             for (const [name,img] of Object.entries(b)) out.push({u,name,anime:ARC_UNIVERSE_ANIME?.[u] || u,img:String(img || '')});
11363:         }
11364:     } catch (_) {}
11365:     return out;
11366: }

11368: async function simplePokeIndex() {
11369:     if (simplePokeIndex.cache) return simplePokeIndex.cache;
11370:     const map = new Map();
11371:     try {
11372:         const r = await fetch('https://pokeapi.co/api/v2/pokemon-species?limit=2000', { headers:{Accept:'application/json'} });
11373:         if (r.ok) {
11374:             const d = await r.json();
11375:             for (const x of d?.results || []) {
11376:                 const id = String(x.url || '').match(/pokemon-species\/(\d+)\/?$/)?.[1];
11377:                 if (id) { map.set(simpleImageNorm(x.name),id); map.set(simpleImageCompact(x.name),id); }
11378:             }
11379:         }
11380:     } catch (_) {}
11381:     simplePokeIndex.cache = map; return map;
11382: }

11686: function __dleIndex(u) {
11687:     if (!__dleNames) __dleNames = new Map();
11688:     const t = DLE_TARGET_NAMES[u] || [], m = DLE_MASTER_NAMES[u] || [];
11689:     const sig = t.length + '|' + m.length;
11690:     let idx = __dleNames.get(u);
11691:     if (idx && idx.sig === sig) return idx;
11692:     const al = DLE_MASTER_ALIASES[u] || {}, display = new Map();
11693:     for (const x of [...t, ...m]) {
11694:         const xn = normalizeDle(x), c = al[xn] || xn;
11695:         if (c && !display.has(c)) display.set(c, x);
11696:     }
11697:     idx = { sig, display };
11698:     __dleNames.set(u, idx);
11699:     return idx;
11700: }

11713: function dleProfileExists(u, raw) {
11714:     const c = dleMasterCanonicalNorm(u, raw);
11715:     if (!c) return false;
11716:     if (DLE_VERIFIED_PROFILES[u]?.has(c)) return true;
11717:     if (!__dleStatic) __dleStatic = new Map();
11718:     let set = __dleStatic.get(u);
11719:     if (!set) {
11720:         set = new Set();
11721:         const base = DLE_UNIVERSES[u];
11722:         const add = name => { const k = dleMasterCanonicalNorm(u, name); if (k) set.add(k); };
11723:         (base?.characters || []).forEach(ch => add(ch.name));
11724:         Object.keys(DLE_STATIC_PROFILES[u] || {}).forEach(name => add(name));

11726:             const [uk, n] = key.split('|');
11727:             if (uk === u && isCompleteDleAttrs(attrs, base?.categories)) add(n);
11728:         }
11729:         __dleStatic.set(u, set);
11730:     }
11731:     return set.has(c);
11732: }

11285: function simpleScoreName(candidate, wanted) {
11286:     const a = simpleImageNorm(candidate), b = simpleImageNorm(wanted);
11287:     if (!a || !b) return 0;
11288:     if (a === b) return 100;
11289:     if (simpleImageCompact(a) === simpleImageCompact(b)) return 98;
11290:     if (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a)) {
11291:         const sm = Math.min(a.length,b.length), lg = Math.max(a.length,b.length);
11292:         if (sm >= 4) return 83 + Math.round(14 * sm / lg);
11293:     }
11294:     const A = new Set(a.split(' ')), B = new Set(b.split(' ')); let common = 0;
11295:     for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) common++;
11296:     return Math.round((common / Math.max(A.size,B.size,1)) * 82);
11297: }

11608: async function startSimpleImageMigration() {
11609:     if (SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.running) return;
11610:     SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.running=true; SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.startedAt=new Date().toISOString(); SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.lastError=null;

11622:     } catch (e) {

11650: const __legacyCharacterImageResolver = resolveCharacterImage;
11651: resolveCharacterImage = async function(universeKey,displayName){
11652:     const name=cleanImageCharacterName(displayName); const normName=normalizeImageKey(name);
11653:     try {
11654:         const r=await pool.query(`SELECT source_url,status,updated_at FROM character_images WHERE universe_key=$1 AND norm_name=$2 AND image_bytes IS NOT NULL AND octet_length(image_bytes)>=700 LIMIT 1`,[universeKey,normName]);
11655:         const row=r.rows[0];
11656:         if (row) return {imageUrl:simpleImageRoute(universeKey,name,new Date(row.updated_at).getTime()),sourceUrl:row.source_url||null,status:'ok'};
11657:     } catch (_) {}
11658:     if (!SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.done) return __legacyCharacterImageResolver(universeKey,displayName);
11659:     return {imageUrl:null,sourceUrl:null,status:'missing'};
11660: };
11661: /* ================= END SIMPLE PERSISTENT CHARACTER IMAGES V1 ============== */
11662: 
11663: server.listen(PORT, () => {
11664:     // Le serveur ouvre le port immédiatement. Les images se remplissent ensuite en arrière-plan.
11665:     setTimeout(() => {
11666:         startSimpleImageMigration().catch(err => console.error('[Simple images] startup migration:', err));
11667:     }, 2500);
11668: 
11669:     // Tant qu'il reste des images manquantes, une nouvelle passe est retentée périodiquement.
11670:     const imageRetryTimer = setInterval(() => {
11671:         if (!SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.running && (!SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.done || SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.missing > 0)) {
11672:             startSimpleImageMigration().catch(err => console.error('[Simple images] scheduled retry:', err));
11673:         }
11674:     }, 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

11234: function simpleImageNorm(value) {

11246: function simpleMimeFromPath(p) {
11247:     const x = String(p || '').toLowerCase();
11248:     if (x.endsWith('.png')) return 'image/png';
11249:     if (x.endsWith('.gif')) return 'image/gif';
11250:     if (x.endsWith('.avif')) return 'image/avif';
11251:     if (x.endsWith('.webp')) return 'image/webp';
11252:     return 'image/jpeg';
11253: }

11255:     return Buffer.isBuffer(buf) && buf.length >= 700 && buf.length <= 10 * 1024 * 1024;
11256: }
11257: function simpleImageIsPrivateHost(hostname) {
11258:     const h = String(hostname || '').toLowerCase();
11259:     if (!h || h === 'localhost' || h === '::1' || h.endsWith('.local')) return true;
11260:     if (/^127\./.test(h) || /^10\./.test(h) || /^192\.168\./.test(h) || /^169\.254\./.test(h)) return true;
11261:     const m = h.match(/^172\.(\d+)\./); if (m && Number(m[1]) >= 16 && Number(m[1]) <= 31) return true;
11262:     return false;
11263: }
11264: async function simpleFetchImage(url, timeoutMs = 14000) {
11265:     if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(url || ''))) return null;
11266:     let parsed;
11267:     try { parsed = new URL(url); } catch (_) { return null; }
11268:     if (simpleImageIsPrivateHost(parsed.hostname)) return null;
11269:     const ac = new AbortController();
11270:     const t = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), timeoutMs);
11271:     try {
11272:         const r = await fetch(url, {
11273:             redirect: 'follow', signal: ac.signal,
11274:             headers: { 'User-Agent':'ANIME-GAME/1.0', 'Accept':'image/avif,image/webp,image/apng,image/*,*/*;q=0.8' }
11275:         });
11276:         if (!r.ok) return null;
11277:         const mime = String(r.headers.get('content-type') || '').split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase();
11278:         if (!mime.startsWith('image/')) return null;
11279:         const bytes = Buffer.from(await r.arrayBuffer());
11280:         if (!simpleImageLooksValid(bytes)) return null;
11281:         return { bytes, mime };
11282:     } catch (_) { return null; }
11283:     finally { clearTimeout(t); }
11284: }
11285: function simpleScoreName(candidate, wanted) {
11286:     const a = simpleImageNorm(candidate), b = simpleImageNorm(wanted);
11287:     if (!a || !b) return 0;
11288:     if (a === b) return 100;
11289:     if (simpleImageCompact(a) === simpleImageCompact(b)) return 98;
11290:     if (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a)) {
11291:         const sm = Math.min(a.length,b.length), lg = Math.max(a.length,b.length);
11292:         if (sm >= 4) return 83 + Math.round(14 * sm / lg);
11293:     }
11294:     const A = new Set(a.split(' ')), B = new Set(b.split(' ')); let common = 0;
11295:     for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) common++;
11296:     return Math.round((common / Math.max(A.size,B.size,1)) * 82);
11297: }

11491:     let cursor=0;
11492:     await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(n,Math.max(1,items.length))}, async()=>{
11493:         while (true) { const i=cursor++; if (i>=items.length) return; await fn(items[i],i); }

11650: const __legacyCharacterImageResolver = resolveCharacterImage;
11651: resolveCharacterImage = async function(universeKey,displayName){
11652:     const name=cleanImageCharacterName(displayName); const normName=normalizeImageKey(name);
11653:     try {
11654:         const r=await pool.query(`SELECT source_url,status,updated_at FROM character_images WHERE universe_key=$1 AND norm_name=$2 AND image_bytes IS NOT NULL AND octet_length(image_bytes)>=700 LIMIT 1`,[universeKey,normName]);
11655:         const row=r.rows[0];
11656:         if (row) return {imageUrl:simpleImageRoute(universeKey,name,new Date(row.updated_at).getTime()),sourceUrl:row.source_url||null,status:'ok'};
11657:     } catch (_) {}
11658:     if (!SIMPLE_IMAGE_STATE.done) return __legacyCharacterImageResolver(universeKey,displayName);
11659:     return {imageUrl:null,sourceUrl:null,status:'missing'};
11660: };
